# UI5 localization audit

Generated by `node scripts/i18n-audit.mjs` from installed UI5 Web Components 2.28.1, UI5 Web Components for React 2.28.2 and OpenUI5 1.154.0. Every native imported key is retained, including rendered subcomponents, function-only announcement helpers and the OpenUI5 library key families required by E5. `gap` means no actual translated render/announcement owner exists; it is never counted as fixed by an unused prop.

The 2026-11-17 E5 scope creates the ratchet in `tests/i18n-audit-baseline.json`; remaining gaps belong to P1, P2, P2a and P3–P6. P2a owns Calendar and the three date pickers; TimePicker and PickerField remain P2. Native source references identify key expressions, not automatic proof of render reachability. Library-definition-only rows still need owning-channel review. `accessibilityInfo.type` is metadata at its direct owner but CustomAnnouncement consumes it in custom List/Table content, so there is no global metadata exclusion. Retained triage categories and notes are carried forward below; they never waive a gap. Full source references, owning English definitions and hashes are preserved in `.integration/E5-evidence/fourth-20261118/audit.json`. English defaults resolve from the native consuming counterpart, including identically named WC and OpenUI5 keys. WCR keys are namespaced as `wcr:<KEY>`. H cards own the WCR inventory, where `reference only` means the translation is available in the pack and the component port is pending.

Pinned-source drift: StepInput delegates to NumericInput in 2.28.1 and uses `NUMBERINPUT_*`, rather than the card example’s earlier `STEPINPUT_*`. Icon has no i18n rows; its zero-count shared display inventory is assigned to P5.
